HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001069/B011"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_05d8ac908df06e94fd22","text":"3. **Present-accounting reversal of accumulated wealth.** Branch evidence: ع ي ن B011, present cash/tangible wealth; ك ث ر B002/B003, wealth/status rivalry and claims; س ء ل B001, questioning; ن ع م B001/B002, favor and ease. Structural cue: the ayah of eye-certainty stands immediately before questioning about نعيم. Assigned role: عين B011 turns previously boasted or absent claims into present, audit-ready substance. Abductive move: I infer an accounting transition: what was counted competitively must become present before interrogation. Reading change: عين اليقين can carry a fiscal/tangible undertone: reality comes due in hand, not as reputation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B001","root_001069/B001","root_001069/B002","root_001696/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_26359f6f83d128950d80","text":"1. **Eye-certainty as face-to-face verification.** Branch evidence: ر ء ي B001, seeing by eye/insight; ع ي ن B001, seeing eye; ع ي ن B002, face-to-face seeing; ي ق ن B001, doubt removed. Structural cue: the pronoun \"ها\" carries الجحيم from 102:6 into a repeated seeing intensified by \"عين اليقين.\" Assigned role: عين supplies directness and يقين supplies settled verification. Abductive move: I infer that the second seeing is not a second object but a higher evidentiary mode. Reading change: the ayah reads as eyewitness certification of what had first been warned and then shown.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001069/B013","root_001696/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2761029f316207ce854e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:7:3\",\"root_001069\",[\"B013\"]],[\"102:7:4\",\"root_001696\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Görülen, bir haber veya temsil değil, kesinliğin gönderme yaptığı şeyin bizzat kendisidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3511f9bcd0dc76eebd4f","text":"102:8 made the present-cash/accounting branch of عين B011 more persuasive than it first appeared. On first pass, \"عين اليقين\" was primarily perceptual; after \"عن النعيم,\" direct certainty also becomes an accounting condition in which what was enjoyed must be present to answer for. The final passive questioning also reinforced the watcher/watched reversal: the eye-certainty scene does not end in seeing but hands the addressees over to interrogation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000225/B003","root_000531/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_485d489cdd0c7c5cf23a","text":"3. **A reciprocal visual confrontation.** Fixed `ر ء ي B004` contributes mutual visibility and facing, and fixed `ع ي ن B001` supplies the seeing organ. Contextual `ج ح م B003` in the immediately preceding object supplies the glowing or staring-eye image of the blaze. Repetition plus `ثُمَّ` keeps viewer and object in sustained confrontation. The fixed eye is assigned human witnessing; the contextual fire-eye is assigned counter-gaze. I infer the reciprocal relation and its personification, since the packet only provides the compatible images. The ayah then makes certainty exposure as well as observation: seeing the blaze also means standing manifest before it.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000225/B003","root_000531/B004","root_001069/B003","root_001069/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_57ffa64c13a15e0902aa","text":"4. **Watcher and watched reverse positions.** Branch evidence: ع ي ن B003, watchful care/supervision; ع ي ن B005, scout/sentinel; ر ء ي B004, mutual visibility/facing; ج ح م B003, staring eye. Structural cue: the ayah repeats seeing after an object with an eye-like blaze branch. Assigned role: عين activates supervision and mutual visibility; جحيم becomes the counter-gaze. Abductive move: I infer that direct sight establishes a field where the viewers themselves are under observation. Reading change: the ayah becomes less about spectators viewing an object and more about being placed inside an observing scene.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001069/B013"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7fbb4f379aba38f6479b","text":"2. **Seeing the exact thing rather than an image or report.** Fixed `ع ي ن B013` supplies “the thing itself,” its exact identity singled out from alternatives. The pronoun `هَا` has an explicit prior referent, and `عَيْنَ` sits between repeated seeing and certainty. Exact identity is assigned the role of excluding substitute, representation, or hearsay. I supply the abductive expansion from lexical exactness to epistemic unmediatedness; the construction itself does not spell out those excluded alternatives. `عَيْنَ ٱلْيَقِينِ` consequently reads as contact with certainty's very referent, not only an especially intense subjective feeling of conviction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B013"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_833b6f932c0fbe10fb2e","text":"102:8 unexpectedly linked `عَيْنَ` to contextual `ن ع م B013`, the accepted eye-gladdening favor formula. That adjacency did not change the direct-certainty reading, but it added a second role for the fixed eye: the faculty forced to witness is also a beneficiary whose pleasures can be gathered into the ensuing question about `ٱلنَّعِيمِ`.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B001","root_001069/B002","root_001696/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_83c7ae036dcb496b55a0","text":"1. **The same object, now encountered face to face.** Fixed `لَتَرَوُنَّهَا` anchors `ر ء ي B001`; fixed `عَيْنَ` anchors `ع ي ن B002` (direct, eyewitness encounter); and fixed `ٱلْيَقِينِ` anchors `ي ق ن B001` (doubt removed by settled knowledge). Initial `ثُمَّ`, the repeated visual verb, and the object pronoun `هَا` structurally reconnect this sight to named `ٱلْجَحِيمَ` while separating it from the first seeing. The three branches serve perception, direct presence, and epistemic settlement respectively. I infer that the second scene deepens mode or proximity rather than changing objects. The ayah thus reads as a second-order encounter in which prior sight becomes unmistakably face-to-face certainty.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001069/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8ee10416ccac8183617a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:7:3\",\"root_001069\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Görmeye zorlanan gözün, daha önce hoşnut edilen bir nimet alıcısı olarak sonraki sorguya dahil edildiği okuması mümkündür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B004","root_001069/B001","root_001069/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a57626e187687bb3fd48","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:7:2\",\"root_000531\",[\"B004\"]],[\"102:7:3\",\"root_001069\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Doğrudan görüş, bakanların da görünür olduğu karşılıklı bir gözetim alanı kurar; seyreden ile seyredilenin konumu tersine döner.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001069/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_da8ebf84246d5f316232","text":"4. **Exploratory: the eye as beneficiary just before benefit is audited.** Fixed `ع ي ن B001` is activated by the next ayah's `ن ع م B013`, whose accepted scope explicitly joins favor with an eye being gladdened or honored. The immediate sequence moves from `عَيْنَ ٱلْيَقِينِ` to questioning about `ٱلنَّعِيمِ`. The eye is assigned a double role: organ of the terrifying verification and recipient of pleasing experience. I infer that sensory enjoyments belong among the favors to be questioned; the weak link is that `B013` is a formulaic branch, not the direct sense of `ٱلنَّعِيمِ`. This activation makes the fixed eye morally implicated: the faculty now compelled to see is also one whose prior gratifications can enter the coming account.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B001","root_001069/B002","root_001696/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e0592b1cde0c32ddbe02","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:7:2\",\"root_000531\",[\"B001\"]],[\"102:7:3\",\"root_001069\",[\"B002\"]],[\"102:7:4\",\"root_001696\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İkinci görme yeni bir nesne sunmaz; aynı gerçeği yüz yüze, kuşkuyu kaldıran daha yüksek bir kanıt düzeyinde karşıya getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001069/B011"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e1a8eccaf10ab4951c3d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:7:3\",\"root_001069\",[\"B011\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Daha önce servet ve statü olarak sayılanlar, sorgudan önce söylenti olmaktan çıkıp elde bulunan ve hesabı verilecek somut varlıklar gibi hazır edilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B001","root_001069/B013","root_001696/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_fe219bfeb667157a108e","text":"2. **The thing itself as certainty.** Branch evidence: ع ي ن B013, the thing itself; ي ق ن B001, settled knowledge; ر ء ي B001, seeing. Structural cue: \"ها\" refers back to الجحيم, while عين can mean exact identity. Assigned role: عين B013 makes the seen \"it itself\" the body of certainty. Abductive move: I infer that certainty here is not an added idea but the exact encounter with the referent.
